{"parentUuid":"dd471cd8-d9b5-4b20-bc92-af9e8c5ebf70","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["b8180b60-3261-4ed2-ac10-a4af4c5c4f6c"],"addedBlocks":["## b8180b60-3261-4ed2-ac10-a4af4c5c4f6c\nThe official Figma MCP server. Use this server whenever the user wants to create, generate, edit, implement, or sync any design, UI, screen, component, mockup, or visual — in Figma, FigJam, Figma Make, or Figma Slides — and whenever the user mentions Figma or provides a figma.com URL.\n\nThis server bridges code and design in both directions, and supports designing from scratch using existing design systems and codebases.\n\nCAPABILITIES:\n- Read designs FROM Figma into code (get_design_context, get_screenshot, get_metadata, get_figjam)\n- Write designs INTO Figma from code, intent, or existing components (use_figma, generate_figma_design, create_new_file, upload_assets)\n- Bridge code and design via Code Connect (get_code_connect_map, add_code_connect_map, list_code_components, get_code_component_info)\n- Create diagrams and FigJam content (generate_diagram, get_figjam)\n\nWHEN TO USE THESE TOOLS:\n- The user wants to create, mock up, or generate any UI, screen, component, or design — even if Figma isn't named\n- The user wants to implement a design as code (design-to-code)\n- The user wants to push a page, view, or component into Figma (code-to-design)\n- The user wants to update, sync, or edit an existing Figma file\n- The user shares a figma.com URL\n- The user wants to build or extend a design system, design tokens, or component library\n- The user wants to create a diagram in FigJam\n\nSKILLS (Prefer the skills shipped with the Figma plugin on the agent being used. If no Figma plugin is installed, use the skills served by the Figma MCP resource):\n- /figma-use — MANDATORY before calling use_figma; fallback: skill://figma/figma-use/SKILL.md\n- /figma-generate-design — for translating an app page or layout into Figma; fallback: skill://figma/figma-generate-design/SKILL.md\n- /figma-generate-library — for building a design system in Figma from code; fallback: skill://figma/figma-generate-library/SKILL.md\n- /figma-code-connect — for mapping Figma components to codebase components; fallback: skill://figma/figma-code-connect/SKILL.md\n"],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"9c705fc1-ec94-46b7-a9cb-8b58abea53ca","timestamp":"2026-06-07T08:09:00.084Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"C:\\Users\\user\\Desktop\\claude_test","sessionId":"e7d255f2-02c9-446d-84b4-f1caec596bf8","version":"2.1.161","gitBranch":"HEAD"}
{"parentUuid":"9c705fc1-ec94-46b7-a9cb-8b58abea53ca","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- gtm-positioning-strategy: Find and own a defensible market position. Use when messaging sounds like competitors, conversion is weak despite awareness, repositioning a product, or testing positioning claims. Includes Crawl-Walk-Run rollout methodology and the word change that improved enterprise deal progression.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- anthropic-skills:consolidate-memory: Reflective pass over your memory files — merge duplicates, fix stale facts, prune the index.\n- anthropic-skills:docx: Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, edit, or manipulate Word documents (.docx files). Triggers include: any mention of 'Word doc', 'word document', '.docx', or requests to produce professional documents with formatting like tables of contents, headings, page numbers, or letterheads. Also use when extracting or reorganizing content from .docx files, inserting or replacing images in documents, performing find-and-replace in Word files, working with tracked changes or comments, or converting content into a polished Word document. If the user asks for a 'report', 'memo', 'letter', 'template', or similar deliverable as a Word or .docx file, use this skill. Do NOT use for PDFs, spreadsheets, Google Docs, or general coding tasks unrelated to document generation.\n- anthropic-skills:pdf: Use this skill whenever the user wants to do anything with PDF files. This includes reading or extracting text/tables from PDFs, combining or merging multiple PDFs into one, splitting PDFs apart, rotating pages, adding watermarks, creating new PDFs, filling PDF forms, encrypting/decrypting PDFs, extracting images, and OCR on scanned PDFs to make them searchable. If the user mentions a .pdf file or asks to produce one, use this skill.\n- anthropic-skills:pptx: Use this skill any time a .pptx file is involved in any way — as input, output, or both. This includes: creating slide decks, pitch decks, or presentations; reading, parsing, or extracting text from any .pptx file (even if the extracted content will be used elsewhere, like in an email or summary); editing, modifying, or updating existing presentations; combining or splitting slide files; working with templates, layouts, speaker notes, or comments. Trigger whenever the user mentions \"deck,\" \"slides,\" \"presentation,\" or references a .pptx filename, regardless of what they plan to do with the content afterward. If a .pptx file needs to be opened, created, or touched, use this skill.\n- anthropic-skills:schedule: Create or update a scheduled task that runs automatically. Use when the user says things like \"every day\", \"each morning\", \"remind me in an hour\", \"run this at noon\", or wants to reschedule an existing task.\n- anthropic-skills:setup-cowork: Guided Cowork setup — install role-matched plugins, connect your tools, try a skill.\n- anthropic-skills:skill-creator: Create new skills, modify and improve existing skills, and measure skill performance. Use when users want to create a skill from scratch, edit, or optimize an existing skill, run evals to test a skill, benchmark skill performance with variance analysis, or optimize a skill's description for better triggering accuracy.\n- anthropic-skills:xlsx: Use this skill any time a spreadsheet file is the primary input or output. This means any task where the user wants to: open, read, edit, or fix an existing .xlsx, .xlsm, .csv, or .tsv file (e.g., adding columns, computing formulas, formatting, charting, cleaning messy data); create a new spreadsheet from scratch or from other data sources; or convert between tabular file formats. Trigger especially when the user references a spreadsheet file by name or path — even casually (like \"the xlsx in my downloads\") — and wants something done to it or produced from it. Also trigger for cleaning or restructuring messy tabular data files (malformed rows, misplaced headers, junk data) into proper spreadsheets. The deliverable must be a spreadsheet file. Do NOT trigger when the primary deliverable is a Word document, HTML report, standalone Python script, database pipeline, or Google Sheets API integration, even if tabular data is involved.\n- productivity:memory-management: Two-tier memory system that makes Claude a true workplace collaborator. Decodes shorthand, acronyms, nicknames, and internal language so Claude understands requests like a colleague would. CLAUDE.md for working memory, memory/ directory for the full knowledge base.\n- productivity:start: Initialize the productivity system and open the dashboard. Use when setting up the plugin for the first time, bootstrapping working memory from your existing task list, or decoding the shorthand (nicknames, acronyms, project codenames) you use in your todos.\n- productivity:task-management: Simple task management using a shared TASKS.md file. Reference this when the user asks about their tasks, wants to add/complete tasks, or needs help tracking commitments.\n- productivity:update: Sync tasks and refresh memory from your current activity. Use when pulling new assignments from your project tracker into TASKS.md, triaging stale or overdue tasks, filling memory gaps for unknown people or projects, or running a comprehensive scan to catch todos buried in chat and email.\n- operations:capacity-plan: Plan resource capacity — workload analysis and utilization forecasting. Use when heading into quarterly planning, the team feels overallocated and you need the numbers, deciding whether to hire or deprioritize, or stress-testing whether upcoming projects fit the people you have.\n- operations:change-request: Create a change management request with impact analysis and rollback plan. Use when proposing a system or process change that needs approval, preparing a change record for CAB review, documenting risk and rollback steps before a deployment, or planning stakeholder communications for a rollout.\n- operations:compliance-tracking: Track compliance requirements and audit readiness. Trigger with \"compliance\", \"audit prep\", \"SOC 2\", \"ISO 27001\", \"GDPR\", \"regulatory requirement\", or when the user needs help tracking, preparing for, or documenting compliance activities.\n- operations:process-doc: Document a business process — flowcharts, RACI, and SOPs. Use when formalizing a process that lives in someone's head, building a RACI to clarify who owns what, writing an SOP for a handoff or audit, or capturing the exceptions and edge cases of how work actually gets done.\n- operations:process-optimization: Analyze and improve business processes. Trigger with \"this process is slow\", \"how can we improve\", \"streamline this workflow\", \"too many steps\", \"bottleneck\", or when the user describes an inefficient process they want to fix.\n- operations:risk-assessment: Identify, assess, and mitigate operational risks. Trigger with \"what are the risks\", \"risk assessment\", \"risk register\", \"what could go wrong\", or when the user is evaluating risks associated with a project, vendor, process, or decision.\n- operations:runbook: Create or update an operational runbook for a recurring task or procedure. Use when documenting a task that on-call or ops needs to run repeatably, turning tribal knowledge into exact step-by-step commands, adding troubleshooting and rollback steps to an existing procedure, or writing escalation paths for when things go wrong.\n- operations:status-report: Generate a status report with KPIs, risks, and action items. Use when writing a weekly or monthly update for leadership, summarizing project health with green/yellow/red status, surfacing risks and decisions that need stakeholder attention, or turning a pile of project tracker activity into a readable narrative.\n- operations:vendor-review: Evaluate a vendor — cost analysis, risk assessment, and recommendation. Use when reviewing a new vendor proposal, deciding whether to renew or replace a contract, comparing two vendors side-by-side, or building a TCO breakdown and negotiation points before procurement sign-off.\n- small-business:business-pulse: Produces a one-page cross-functional business snapshot for SMB owners — cash position (QuickBooks), sales trend (PayPal/Square), pipeline movement (HubSpot), this week's commitments (Calendar), urgent watch-list items (Gmail/Slack), and the single most important thing needing attention today. Proactively tries every available connector and gracefully scopes to whatever is connected — one connector gives a partial pulse; the full stack gives the full picture. Trigger when the user asks how the business is doing, wants a snapshot, a weekly summary, a Monday brief, or says anything like \"what am I missing\" or \"catch me up on the business.\"\n- small-business:call-list: Ranks the top-5 leads most worth calling today, supplies talking points from email history, blocks time on the calendar, and drafts follow-up messages. Accepts optional count and date arguments.\n- small-business:canva-creator: Takes an approved content brief and executes a campaign end-to-end: builds the posting calendar, generates Canva designs for social posts, drafts caption and email copy, and stages social sends in HubSpot. Canva is used for social posts only (Instagram, Facebook, X, LinkedIn) — email content is drafted as plain text and surfaced inline for the owner to send from their own tool. Every step requires explicit owner approval. Use when the user says \"make the content,\" \"generate the posts,\" \"create the assets,\" \"turn this into a campaign,\" or hands off an approved brief for execution.\n- small-business:cash-flow-snapshot: Reads AR/AP, historical cash timing, and known fixed costs from QuickBooks, PayPal, Stripe, or Square — or a CSV upload — and produces a 30/60/90-day cash flow forecast with percentage-variance confidence bands and named risk flags. Delivers a chat summary and a downloadable XLSX. Use when the user asks \"forecast my cash flow,\" \"will I make payroll,\" mentions \"runway,\" or says \"cash crunch.\" Falls back to CSV upload when no connector is live.\n- small-business:close-month: Closes the month — reconciles QB vs payment processors, flags gaps, writes P&L narrative, exports close packet. Accepts optional month and save-to arguments.\n- small-business:content-strategy: Analyzes sales data from PayPal and QuickBooks to find top performers and slow movers, layers in seasonality, and produces a prioritized 30-day content brief: what to push, what offers to run, what to hold. Strategic output only — no calendars or assets. Use when the user asks what to post, wants a content plan, asks what's selling, or what to promote this month.\n- small-business:contract-review: Lightweight NDA, MSA, and vendor contract review for SMBs without legal on staff. Reads contracts from local files, Gmail attachments, or DocuSign envelopes; flags non-standard terms; explains risks in plain English; and outputs a marked-up redline as a separate DOCX. Use when the user says \"review this contract,\" \"what am I signing,\" \"red flags,\" \"flag any concerns,\" \"check the payment terms,\" or uploads/forwards a contract or legal agreement.\n- small-business:crm-cleanup: Scans HubSpot for stale deals, duplicate contacts, and missing fields, then fixes what the owner approves. Accepts optional scope argument for deals, contacts, or all.\n- small-business:crm-maintenance: Keeps HubSpot current without the owner opening it: creates and updates contacts and deals from email and calendar context, logs notes and calls, and flags stale records. The \"stop doing data entry\" skill. Use when the user asks to update the CRM, log a call, clean up HubSpot, or add context to a deal.\n- small-business:customer-pulse: Aggregates PayPal disputes, HubSpot feedback and tickets, and email sentiment (plus pasted or exported Google/Yelp reviews) into a themes report with verbatim evidence and a \"do these three things this week\" list. Use when the user asks how customers are feeling, for review analysis, what people are saying, or about disputes.\n- small-business:customer-pulse-check: Synthesizes themes from PayPal disputes, HubSpot tickets, and review exports into a top-3 fixable issues list with drafted response templates. Accepts optional since-date argument.\n- small-business:friday-brief: Delivers the Friday end-of-week pulse — revenue vs prior week, top sellers, wins and watches. Accepts optional lookback window of 7 or 14 days.\n- small-business:handle-complaint: Handles an incoming customer complaint end-to-end — pulls context, drafts a response, and suggests an operational fix. Accepts optional email or ticket ID argument.\n- small-business:invoice-chase: Drafts overdue-invoice reminder emails from QuickBooks and PayPal data, matched to each customer's payment history and tone (gentle for good customers, firm for repeat late payers). Sends via PayPal with owner approval; non-PayPal invoices queue as mail drafts. Use when the user asks \"who owes me money,\" mentions overdue invoices, or wants to follow up on unpaid invoices.\n- small-business:job-post-builder: Builds end-to-end hiring packets — job post, structured interview guide with scoring rubric, and offer letter template — from a hiring brief. Triggers on: \"help me hire\", \"we're hiring for\", \"write a job post\", \"job description\", \"JD\", \"open role\", \"create a job ad\", \"interview questions\", \"scoring rubric\", \"draft an offer letter\", \"send an offer\", \"make a hiring packet\", or any request to recruit for a position. When in doubt, trigger — covers the full hiring workflow from job post through DocuSign envelope creation via browser. Does NOT screen or rank applicants.\n- small-business:lead-triage: Scores inbound HubSpot leads by engagement signals, company fit, and urgency markers to produce a \"call these 5 today\" list with talking points, drafts the follow-ups, and blocks Calendar time. Use when the user asks to prioritize leads, who to call first, or about their pipeline.\n- small-business:margin-analyzer: Analyzes unit economics by product or service using PayPal merchant insights and QuickBooks cost data, benchmarks against inflation and cost changes, and shows pricing-scenario data (e.g. \"a 5% increase historically correlates with ~3% volume drop\"). Surfaces analysis only — does not recommend a price. Use when the user asks about raising prices, pricing, margin analysis, what to charge, whether costs are eating into profit, or how a price change might affect their business. Trigger even if the user doesn't say \"margin\" explicitly — phrases like \"am I making enough?\", \"should I charge more?\", or \"my costs are going up\" all call for this skill.\n- small-business:monday-brief: Generates a one-page Monday morning briefing — cash, sales, pipeline, week ahead, top three to-dos. Accepts optional post destination and save-to arguments.\n- small-business:month-end-prep: Walks an SMB owner through month-end close: reconciles QuickBooks against PayPal (and Square/Stripe) settlements, flags uncategorized transactions, suspicious duplicates, and missing receipts, then writes a plain-English P&L narrative and exports a close packet (xlsx + one-page PDF). Use when the user says \"close the month,\" \"month-end,\" \"reconcile,\" \"what's missing,\" \"P&L,\" or asks why revenue or margin changed this month.\n- small-business:month-heads-up: Runs on the 25th — shows the next 30-day cash-flow outlook and flags anything that needs attention before month-end. Accepts optional 30 or 60 day horizon.\n- small-business:plan-payroll: Forecasts cash, ranks overdue invoices, and stages PayPal reminders so the owner can confidently run payroll. Accepts optional horizon and payroll-date arguments.\n- small-business:price-check: Produces a margin-by-product table and three pricing-scenario data views so the owner can see the full financial picture before making a pricing decision. Accepts optional product name argument.\n- small-business:quarterly-review: Generates a full QBR narrative — revenue trend, margin trend, customer health, top opportunities and risks — as a presentation-ready PDF or deck. Accepts optional quarter and save-to arguments.\n- small-business:review-contract: Reviews a contract in plain English, surfaces red flags with severity ratings, and produces a marked-up docx/PDF with suggested redlines. Accepts optional file path or DocuSign envelope ID.\n- small-business:run-campaign: Runs an end-to-end marketing campaign — sales analysis, content brief, Canva assets, HubSpot send. Accepts optional lookback and channel arguments.\n- small-business:sales-brief: Surfaces top and bottom sellers, identifies seasonality patterns, and produces a 2-week content brief to push winners and clear slow movers. Accepts optional lookback window of 30, 60, or 90 days.\n- small-business:smb-onboard: Claude as the trainer. Walks an SMB owner through connecting their first two tools, runs one recipe to prove immediate value, interviews them about their business (industry, size, top three headaches), stores that context persistently so every other skill benefits, and sets a weekly check-in cadence. Use when the owner is getting started or says any of: \"set me up,\" \"setup,\" \"help me get set up,\" \"get started,\" \"help me get started,\" \"get me started,\" \"what can you do,\" \"I'm new to this,\" or is in their first session.\n- small-business:smb-router: The front door to the Small Business plugin. Listens to what the owner needs right now — vague or specific — and routes them to the best skill or slash command for the moment. Also serves as a guide: explains what's available, suggests what to try next, and adapts recommendations based on stored business context. Trigger whenever the owner asks \"what can you do,\" \"help me with my business,\" \"what should I focus on,\" \"I don't know where to start,\" or any open-ended business request that doesn't clearly match a single skill.\n- small-business:tax-prep: Prepares tax-season materials — quarterly estimated tax calculation or year-end 1099 prep — and produces an accountant handoff packet. Accepts optional mode and year arguments.\n- small-business:tax-season-organizer: Prepares tax-season materials for small business owners — framed as deliverables for their accountant, not tax advice. Two modes: (1) quarterly estimated tax calculation — pulls YTD net income from QuickBooks and calculates the federal income tax + self-employment tax liability and quarterly payment due; (2) year-end 1099 prep — scans QuickBooks, PayPal, and Stripe for contractors paid over $600, builds a 1099-NEC candidate list with missing W-9 flags, and produces a plain-English summary a CPA can work from directly.\nTrigger this skill whenever the user mentions: quarterly taxes, estimated tax payment, how much to set aside for taxes, 1099s, 1099-NEC, year-end tax prep, contractor payments, W-9s, or any phrase suggesting they are preparing for a tax deadline or handing materials to an accountant. Also trigger proactively when a user asks about net profit or YTD income in a context that suggests they are worried about their tax bill.\n- small-business:ticket-deflector: Reads a forwarded customer email or ticket, pulls order/refund status from PayPal and account history from HubSpot, drafts a tone-matched reply in the owner's writing voice, and can issue a PayPal refund with explicit owner approval. Use when the user says \"draft a response,\" \"answer this customer,\" \"where's my order,\" or \"I want a refund.\"\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by running the app and observing behavior. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current {"parentUuid":"0e3f13a4-80f6-46cc-9fda-4a6c3669c32a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"b7e2c558-1683-44f2-9ce4-33bfee490894","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_015DP3TJY2UzAmu3MmsBARtE","content":"Launching skill: loop"}]},"uuid":"2a54e7c1-bc89-4b63-aeea-1942501be12d","timestamp":"2026-06-07T08:10:35.793Z","toolUseResult":{"success":true,"commandName":"loop"},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0e3f13a4-80f6-46cc-9fda-4a6c3669c32a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"C:\\Users\\user\\Desktop\\claude_test","sessionId":"e7d255f2-02c9-446d-84b4-f1caec596bf8","version":"2.1.161","gitBranch":"HEAD"}
{"parentUuid":"2a54e7c1-bc89-4b63-aeea-1942501be12d","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"b7e2c558-1683-44f2-9ce4-33bfee490894","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# /loop — schedule a recurring or self-paced prompt\n\nParse the input below into `[interval] <prompt…>` and schedule it.\n\n## Parsing (in priority order)\n\n1. **Leading token**: if the first whitespace-delimited token matches `^\\d+[smhd]$` (e.g. `5m`, `2h`), that's the interval; the rest is the prompt.\n2. **Trailing \"every\" clause**: otherwise, if the input ends with `every <N><unit>` or `every <N> <unit-word>` (e.g. `every 20m`, `every 5 minutes`, `every 2 hours`), extract that as the interval and strip it from the prompt. Only match when what follows \"every\" is a time expression — `check every PR` has no interval.\n3. **No interval**: otherwise, the entire input is the prompt and you'll self-pace dynamically (see \"Dynamic mode\" below).\n\nIf the resulting prompt is empty, show usage `/loop [interval] <prompt>` and stop.\n\nExamples:\n- `5m /babysit-prs` → interval `5m`, prompt `/babysit-prs` (rule 1)\n- `check the deploy every 20m` → interval `20m`, prompt `check the deploy` (rule 2)\n- `run tests every 5 minutes` → interval `5m`, prompt `run tests` (rule 2)\n- `check the deploy` → no interval → dynamic mode, prompt `check the deploy` (rule 3)\n- `check every PR` → no interval → dynamic mode, prompt `check every PR` (rule 3 — \"every\" not followed by time)\n- `5m` → empty prompt → show usage\n\n## Offer cloud first\n\nBefore any scheduling step, check whether EITHER is true:\n- the parsed interval (rule 1 or 2) is **≥60 minutes**, or\n- regardless of which rule matched, the original input uses daily phrasing (\"every morning\", \"daily\", \"every day\", \"each night\", \"every weekday\")\n\nIf either is true, call AskUserQuestion first:\n- `question`: \"This loop stops when you close this session. Set it up as a cloud schedule instead so it keeps running?\"\n- `header`: \"Schedule\"\n- `options`: `[{label: \"Cloud schedule (recommended)\", description: \"Runs in Anthropic's cloud even after you close this session\"}, {label: \"This session only\", description: \"Runs in this terminal until you exit\"}]`\n\nIf they pick **Cloud schedule**: do NOT call CronCreate. Invoke the `schedule` skill directly via the Skill tool with `args` set to their original input verbatim (e.g. `Skill({skill: \"schedule\", args: \"every morning tell me a joke\"})`), then follow that skill's instructions to completion. Do NOT tell the user to run /schedule themselves. **Then stop — do not continue to any section below** (no CronCreate, no ScheduleWakeup, no \"execute the prompt now\").\nIf they pick **This session only**:\n- If the trigger was a parsed ≥60-minute interval (rule 1 or 2): continue below with that interval.\n- If the trigger was daily phrasing only (rule 3, no parsed interval): do NOT call CronCreate. Explain that a daily-cadence loop won't fire before this session closes, so there's nothing useful to schedule locally — suggest they either pick Cloud schedule, or re-run `/loop` with an explicit shorter interval (e.g. `/loop 1h <prompt>`) if they want a session loop. Then stop.\nIf neither trigger condition was met: continue below.\n\n## Fixed-interval mode (rules 1 and 2)\n\nConvert the interval to a cron expression:\n\n| Interval pattern      | Cron expression     | Notes                                    |\n|-----------------------|---------------------|------------------------------------------|\n| `Nm` where N ≤ 59   | `*/N * * * *`     | every N minutes                          |\n| `Nm` where N ≥ 60   | `0 */H * * *`     | round to hours (H = N/60, must divide 24)|\n| `Nh` where N ≤ 23   | `0 */N * * *`     | every N hours                            |\n| `Nd`                | `0 0 */N * *`     | every N days at midnight local           |\n| `Ns`                | treat as `ceil(N/60)m` | cron minimum granularity is 1 minute  |\n\n**If the interval doesn't cleanly divide its unit** (e.g. `7m` → `*/7 * * * *` gives uneven gaps at :56→:00; `90m` → 1.5h which cron can't express), pick the nearest clean interval and tell the user what you rounded to before scheduling.\n\nThen:\n1. Call CronCreate with: `cron` (the expression above), `prompt` (the parsed prompt verbatim), `recurring: true`.\n2. Briefly confirm: what's scheduled, the cron expression, the human-readable cadence, that recurring tasks auto-expire after 7 days, and that the user can cancel sooner with CronDelete (include the job ID). Only if you did NOT show the cloud-offer AskUserQuestion above (i.e., neither trigger condition applied), end the confirmation with this exact line on its own, italicized: `_Runs until you close this session · For durable cloud-based loops, use /schedule_`. If the user already answered that question, omit this line.\n3. **Then immediately execute the parsed prompt now** — don't wait for the first cron fire. If it's a slash command, invoke it via the Skill tool; otherwise act on it directly.\n\n## Dynamic mode (rule 3 — no interval)\n\nThe user wants you to self-pace. Decide what makes the next iteration worth running — a passage of time, or an observable event.\n\n1. **Run the parsed prompt now.** If it's a slash command, invoke it via the Skill tool; otherwise act on it directly.\n2. **If the next run is gated on an event** (CI finishing, a log line matching, a file changing, a PR comment) and no Monitor is already running for it: arm one now with `persistent: true`. Its events arrive as `<task-notification>` messages and wake this loop immediately — you do not wait for the ScheduleWakeup deadline. Arm once; on later iterations call TaskList first and skip this step if a monitor is already running.\n3. **Briefly confirm**: that you're self-pacing, whether a Monitor is the primary wake signal, that you ran the task now, and what fallback delay you're about to pick. Write this as text *before* calling ScheduleWakeup — the turn ends as soon as that tool returns.\n4. **Then, as the last action of this turn, call ScheduleWakeup** with:\n   - `delaySeconds`: with a Monitor armed this is the **fallback heartbeat** — how long to wait if no event fires (lean 1200–1800s; idle ticks past the 5-minute cache window are pure overhead). Without a Monitor this is the cadence — pick based on what you observed. Read the tool's own description for cache-aware delay guidance.\n   - `reason`: one short sentence on why you picked that delay.\n   - `prompt`: the full original /loop input verbatim, prefixed with `/loop ` so the next firing re-enters this skill and continues the loop. For example, if the user typed `/loop check the deploy`, pass `/loop check the deploy` as the prompt.\n5. **If you were woken by a `<task-notification>`** rather than this prompt: handle the event in the context of the loop task, then call ScheduleWakeup again with the same `prompt` and the same 1200–1800s `delaySeconds` from step 4 — the Monitor remains the wake signal; this only resets the safety net.\n6. **To stop the loop**, omit the ScheduleWakeup call and TaskStop any Monitor you armed (use TaskList to find the task ID if it is no longer in context).\n\n## Input\n\npremiummultishop.com 사이트에 대해 SEO를 수행. 내부 제품 페이지와 사이트 구석구석을 계속 살펴보며 SEO 개선 작업 진행"}]},"isMeta":true,"uuid":"d14a4009-c7a4-4355-9895-d196807ccda8","timestamp":"2026-06-07T08:10:35.771Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_015DP3TJY2UzAmu3MmsBARtE","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"C:\\Users\\user\\Desktop\\claude_test","sessionId":"e7d255f2-02c9-446d-84b4-f1caec596bf8","version":"2.1.161","gitBranch":"HEAD"}

{"parentUuid":"9d6145be-a619-4117-b72c-bfee1bca5682","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"ab9d209d-f66b-4cce-a0af-c8fc07730f12","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UpCFy3zq7FKFUT2yirpWPk","type":"tool_result","content":"# Product Page SEO Skill - Complete Contents\n\n```\n---\nname: product-page-seo\ndescription: \"Optimize e-commerce product pages for search engine visibility. On-page SEO, structured data, page speed, mobile optimization, and content strategy for Google, Bing, and platform-specific search.\"\nmetadata:\n  nexscope:\n    emoji: \"🔎\"\n    category: ecommerce\n---\n\n# Product Page SEO 🔎\n\nOptimize e-commerce product pages for search engine visibility. On-page SEO, structured data, page speed, mobile optimization, and content strategy for Google, Bing, and platform-specific search.\n\n**Supported platforms:** Amazon, Shopify, WooCommerce, Walmart, TikTok Shop, Etsy, eBay, BigCommerce.\n\nBuilt by [Nexscope](https://www.nexscope.ai/?co-from=skill) — your AI assistant for smarter e-commerce decisions.\n\n## Install\n\n```bash\nnpx skills add nexscope-ai/eCommerce-Skills --skill product-page-seo -g\n```\n\n## Usage\n\n```\nAudit the SEO of my Shopify product page for a yoga mat. URL: [paste URL]. I'm getting zero organic traffic from Google.\n```\n\n## Capabilities\n\n- Product page SEO audit framework (title tag, meta description, H1, URL)\n- Structured data markup (Product schema, Review schema, FAQ schema)\n- Image optimization (alt text, file size, WebP conversion)\n- Page speed optimization recommendations\n- Mobile usability optimization\n- Internal linking strategy for product pages\n- Content enrichment (FAQs, buying guides, comparison tables)\n\n## How This Skill Works\n\n**Step 1:** Collect information from the user's message — product, platform, current situation, and goals.\n\n**Step 2:** Ask one follow-up with all remaining questions using multiple-choice format. Allow shorthand answers (e.g., \"1b 2c 3a\").\n\n**Step 3:** Research and analyze using the frameworks and methodology below.\n\n**Step 4:** Deliver structured, actionable output with specific recommendations, not vague advice.\n\n## Output Format\n\n- Start with a summary of findings\n- Include specific data points and benchmarks where available\n- Provide prioritized action items\n- Mark estimates with ⚠️ when based on incomplete data\n- End with concrete next steps\n\n## Other Skills\n\nMore e-commerce skills: [nexscope-ai/eCommerce-Skills](https://github.com/nexscope-ai/eCommerce-Skills)\n\nAmazon-specific skills: [nexscope-ai/Amazon-Skills](https://github.com/nexscope-ai/Amazon-Skills)\n\nBuilt by [Nexscope](https://www.nexscope.ai/?co-from=skill) — your AI assistant for smarter e-commerce decisions.\n```"}]},"uuid":"49d14cc8-b944-47f9-b264-7b0b3282aaa3","timestamp":"2026-06-07T08:12:46.075Z","toolUseResult":{"bytes":2414,"code":200,"codeText":"OK","result":"# Product Page SEO Skill - Complete Contents\n\n```\n---\nname: product-page-seo\ndescription: \"Optimize e-commerce product pages for search engine visibility. On-page SEO, structured data, page speed, mobile optimization, and content strategy for Google, Bing, and platform-specific search.\"\nmetadata:\n  nexscope:\n    emoji: \"🔎\"\n    category: ecommerce\n---\n\n# Product Page SEO 🔎\n\nOptimize e-commerce product pages for search engine visibility. On-page SEO, structured data, page speed, mobile optimization, and content strategy for Google, Bing, and platform-specific search.\n\n**Supported platforms:** Amazon, Shopify, WooCommerce, Walmart, TikTok Shop, Etsy, eBay, BigCommerce.\n\nBuilt by [Nexscope](https://www.nexscope.ai/?co-from=skill) — your AI assistant for smarter e-commerce decisions.\n\n## Install\n\n```bash\nnpx skills add nexscope-ai/eCommerce-Skills --skill product-page-seo -g\n```\n\n## Usage\n\n```\nAudit the SEO of my Shopify product page for a yoga mat. URL: [paste URL]. I'm getting zero organic traffic from Google.\n```\n\n## Capabilities\n\n- Product page SEO audit framework (title tag, meta description, H1, URL)\n- Structured data markup (Product schema, Review schema, FAQ schema)\n- Image optimization (alt text, file size, WebP conversion)\n- Page speed optimization recommendations\n- Mobile usability optimization\n- Internal linking strategy for product pages\n- Content enrichment (FAQs, buying guides, comparison tables)\n\n## How This Skill Works\n\n**Step 1:** Collect information from the user's message — product, platform, current situation, and goals.\n\n**Step 2:** Ask one follow-up with all remaining questions using multiple-choice format. {"parentUuid":"e29ae988-a533-44e6-92b4-925d28e6692b","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_016CPwrrPLzQUHgggVMuX4bA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TNEEqp9Y7rkfo4cvPzfWG1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\Users\\user\\Desktop\\claude_test\\premiummultishop-seo\\SEO-진단리포트-1차.md","content":"# 프리미엄멀티샵 기술 SEO 진단 리포트 (1차)\n\n- **대상 사이트:** https://www.premiummultishop.com/ (Cafe24 기반 명품 편집샵)\n- **진단일:** 2026-06-07\n- **목표(사용자 지정):** 기술 SEO 기반 정비 / 현황: 유입 정체 / 타겟: 국내(한국) 중심\n- **사용 스킬:** `product-page-seo` (nexscope-ai/ecommerce-skills)\n- **이번 회차 점검 범위:** 홈페이지, 제품 상세 1건(88336), 카테고리 1건(신상/25), robots.txt, sitemap(2개)\n\n---\n\n## 1. 요약 (Executive Summary)\n\n전반적으로 **기초 토대는 양호**합니다. URL이 한글 슬러그 기반으로 SEO 친화적이고, 사이트맵에 **2,710개 URL**(카테고리 1,508 + 제품 1,202)이 등록돼 있으며, 제품 상세에 **Product 구조화 데이터가 이미 존재**하고, 메타 디스크립션이 상품별로 자동 생성되어 품질이 좋습니다. `lang=\"ko\"`, canonical, GA·네이버·구글 인증도 설치돼 있습니다.\n\n그러나 **head 영역에 사이트 전역으로 깔리는 치명적 결함 3가지**가 발견됐고, 이것이 \"유입 정체\"의 숨은 원인일 가능성이 큽니다. 특히 **GA4가 2개 동시 설치**돼 있어 트래픽 측정 자체가 왜곡됐을 수 있습니다 — 즉 \"정체\"라는 진단의 근거 데이터부터 신뢰도 점검이 필요합니다.\n\n### 한눈에 보는 우선순위\n\n| 순위 | 이슈 | 영향 | 난이도 |\n|------|------|------|--------|\n| 🔴 P0 | 중복·깨진 Open Graph 태그 (전역) | SNS 공유·소셜 미리보기 전부 깨짐, 검색엔진에 모순 신호 | 낮음 |\n| 🔴 P0 | GA4 이중 측정 (측정ID 2개) | 트래픽·전환 데이터 신뢰도 붕괴 | 낮음 |\n| 🔴 P0 | JSON-LD `@type:Person` 오류 (전역) | 브랜드를 사람으로 오인식, Organization 신호 분산 | 낮음 |\n| 🟠 P1 | www / non-www 혼재 | 링크 가치 분산, 색인 혼선 | 중간 |\n| 🟠 P1 | Product 스키마 보강 (별점/리뷰·sku·브레드크럼) | 검색결과 별점·리치결과 미노출 = CTR 손실 | 중간 |\n| 🟠 P1 | 카테고리 title 중복(\"신상 - 신상\"), 홈 title 빈약 | 키워드 기회 손실 | 낮음 |\n| 🟠 P1 | 이미지 alt 누락 (상품상세 22/44개) | 이미지 검색(특히 네이버) 유입 손실 | 중간 |\n| 🟡 P2 | 중복 인증태그·meta keywords·파라미터 URL·사이트맵 저가치 페이지 | 위생 수준 | 낮음 |\n\n---\n\n## 2. 잘 되어 있는 점 (유지)\n\n- ✅ **SEO 친화 URL**: `/product/데우스엑스마키나-쉘-남성-자켓-dmf246595-blk/88336/` — 한글 키워드 + 브랜드 + 모델코드 포함. 매우 좋음.\n- ✅ **사이트맵 인덱스 구성**: `sitemap.xml` → `sitemap0/1.xml.gz`, 총 2,710 URL, 최근(2026-06-06) 갱신.\n- ✅ **제품별 메타 디스크립션 자동 생성**: 키워드·정품·배송 메시지 포함, 품질 우수.\n  - 예: *\"PREMIUMMULTISHOP | 데우스엑스마키나 쉘 남성 자켓으로 스타일과 품격을 더하세요. 검증된 정품, 빠른 배송…\"*\n- ✅ **Product 구조화 데이터 존재**: `@type:Product` + `offers`(가격/통화/재고/URL) + `brand`.\n- ✅ **canonical 태그 존재** (홈·제품·카테고리 모두), 제품/카테고리는 pretty URL로 정확히 지정.\n- ✅ **단일 H1** (제품 상세): `<h1>데우스엑스마키나 쉘 남성 자켓 DMF246595 BLK</h1>` — 구조 적절.\n- ✅ `lang=\"ko\"`, viewport, GA4·네이버·구글·페이스북 인증 설치.\n- ✅ robots.txt에 관리/주문/결제/로그인 경로 차단, 네이버(Yeti)·Bing crawl-delay 설정, 사이트맵 명시.\n\n---\n\n## 3. 상세 진단 및 조치\n\n### 🔴 P0-1. 중복·깨진 Open Graph 태그 (전역, 모든 페이지)\n\n모든 페이지 head에 **OG 태그가 두 벌** 들어 있고, 첫 번째(커스텀 삽입) 블록이 깨져 있습니다.\n\n증거 (홈 `home.html` 1336–1342행, 제품 1394–1400행에서 동일 반복):\n```html\n<meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"website\">\n<meta property=\"og:title\" content=\"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\">\n<meta property=\"og:description\" content=\"프리미엄멀티샵은 상품만 판매하는 곳이 아닌, 가치와 신뢰를 판매합니다\">\n<meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"https://blue3200.cafe24.com/web/upload/share-image-1-...png\">  <!-- ❌ og:url에 이미지·스테이징 도메인 -->\n<meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"https://www.premiummultishop.com\"><!-- Cafe24 SEO -->\n```\n문제점:\n1. `og:url`이 **이미지 파일**을 가리킴 (정상값은 페이지 URL).\n2. `og:url`에 **Cafe24 기본 도메인 `blue3200.cafe24.com`**(스테이징/임시 도메인)이 노출 — 외부에 운영 도메인이 아닌 주소가 새어 나감.\n3. 같은 페이지에 `og:title`·`og:url`·`og:type`이 **각각 2~4회 중복**되어 Cafe24 자동 SEO 블록(올바른 값)과 충돌.\n\n**조치:** 디자인 편집(HTML)에서 이 커스텀 OG 블록을 **삭제**하고, Cafe24가 자동 생성하는 `<!-- Cafe24 SEO -->` 블록만 남기세요. (아래 §4 스니펫의 표준 OG 블록 참고)\n\n---\n\n### 🔴 P0-2. GA4 이중 측정 (측정ID 2개 동시 작동)\n\n홈 head에 GA4가 **두 번** 설치돼 서로 다른 측정ID로 동시 전송됩니다.\n```html\n<script async src=\"https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=G-XVP0MPE74Y\"></script>  <!-- 1311행 -->\n<script async src=\"https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=G-CM2XVZ6X45\"></script>  <!-- 1326행 -->\n```\n**영향:** 페이지뷰·세션·전환이 두 속성에 분산/중복 기록됩니다. \"유입 정체\"라는 판단 자체가 잘못된 데이터에서 나왔을 수 있으므로, **SEO 작업 전에 측정부터 정상화**해야 효과를 정확히 검증할 수 있습니다.\n\n**조치:** 실제로 보는 GA4 속성 **하나만 남기고** 나머지 gtag 스니펫 제거. (GTM을 쓰는 경우 GTM 한 곳으로 일원화)\n\n---\n\n### 🔴 P0-3. JSON-LD `@type:Person` 오류 (전역)\n\n전 페이지에 브랜드를 **사람(Person)** 으로 선언한 구조화 데이터가 있습니다.\n```json\n{ \"@context\":\"http://schema.org\", \"@type\":\"Person\", \"name\":\"프리미엄멀티샵\",\n  \"url\":\"https://premiummultishop.com/\",\n  \"sameAs\":[\"instagram…\",\"facebook…\",\"youtube…\"] }\n```\n그리고 **별도로** `@type:Organization` 블록이 또 있습니다(연락처·주소 포함). 즉 회사를 가리키는 신호가 **Person + Organization 두 개로 쪼개져** 있고, 사회관계망(sameAs)은 잘못된 Person 쪽에 붙어 있습니다.\n\n**조치:** Person 블록 삭제 → 하나의 **Organization**(또는 명품 리테일러이므로 `Store`)으로 통합하고 `logo`·`sameAs`를 합치세요. 추가로 **WebSite + SearchAction** 스키마를 넣으면 브랜드 검색 시 사이트링크 검색창이 노출될 수 있습니다. (§4 스니펫)\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 P1-4. www / non-www 도메인 혼재\n\n- 사이트는 **`www.`** 로 접속되는데, canonical·hreflang·og:url은 **non-www**(`https://premiummultishop.com/`)를 가리킴.\n- og:image는 또 `www.` 도메인 사용 → 호스트 표기 불일치.\n\n**영향:** 검색엔진이 정규 호스트를 헷갈리면 링크 평가가 분산될 수 있습니다.\n**조치:**\n1. 대표 도메인 1개 결정(권장: canonical이 가리키는 **non-www** 또는 운영상 쓰는 **www** 중 택1).\n2. 나머지 → 대표 도메인으로 **301 리다이렉트** 강제(Cafe24 멀티도메인/대표도메인 설정).\n3. canonical·og:url·og:image·sitemap 호스트를 **전부 동일하게** 통일.\n4. Search Console·네이버 서치어드바이저에 대표 도메인 등록.\n> ⚠️ 리다이렉트 동작은 서버 설정이라 외부에서 단정 불가 — 실제 301 적용 여부 확인 필요(다음 회차 점검 예정).\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 P1-5. Product 구조화 데이터 보강\n\n현재 Product 스키마(제품 88336)에는 `name·image·description·brand·offers`가 있으나 **리치 결과의 핵심 요소가 빠져** 있습니다.\n\n| 항목 | 현재 | 권장 |\n|------|------|------|\n| `aggregateRating` / `review` | ❌ 없음 | ✅ 추가 (검색결과 **별점** 노출 → CTR↑) |\n| `sku` / `mpn` | ❌ 없음 | ✅ 모델코드(DMF246595) 매핑 |\n| `BreadcrumbList` | ❌ 없음 | ✅ 카테고리 경로 → **브레드크럼 리치결과** |\n| `offers.availability` | `\"InStock\"` | `\"https://schema.org/InStock\"` (정식 표기) |\n| `offers.url` | `?product_no=` 파라미터 | canonical(pretty) URL과 일치 권장 |\n| `itemCondition` | ❌ | `https://schema.org/NewCondition` |\n\n제품 페이지에 **리뷰 섹션(`<h2>리뷰</h2>`)이 실제로 존재**하므로, 집계 평점을 `aggregateRating`으로 연결하면 가장 효과가 큽니다. (§4 스니펫)\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 P1-6. 타이틀 최적화\n\n- **홈 title**: `<title>프리미엄멀티샵</title>` — 브랜드명만. 키워드 0개.\n  - 권장 예: `프리미엄멀티샵 | 명품 편집샵·정품 보장·당일배송`\n- **카테고리 title**: `<title>신상 - 신상 - PREMIUMMULTISHOP</title>` — **\"신상\"이 중복**. 템플릿이 카테고리명을 두 번 출력.\n  - 권장 예: `신상품 | 프리미엄멀티샵` 또는 `신상 명품 신상품 모음 | 프리미엄멀티샵`\n- 제품 title은 양호: `데우스엑스마키나 DMF246595 BLK 남성 자켓 | 프리미엄멀티샵 추천 상품` ✅\n\n**조치:** Cafe24 디자인 편집의 타이틀 템플릿 점검 — 카테고리 중복 변수 제거, 홈 타이틀에 핵심 키워드(명품/편집샵/정품) 반영.\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 P1-7. 이미지 alt 텍스트 누락\n\n| 페이지 | 총 이미지 | alt 속성 자체 없음 | 빈 alt(`alt=\"\"`) |\n|--------|-----------|---------------------|-------------------|\n| 홈 | 209 | 10 | 48 |\n| **제품 상세(88336)** | 44 | **22** | 12 |\n| 카테고리(신상) | 1,018 | 0 | 205 |\n\n- 제품 상세는 **이미지의 절반(22개)에 alt 속성이 아예 없음** — 상세 설명 이미지로 추정. 국내는 **네이버 이미지 검색** 유입 비중이 커서 손실이 큽니다.\n- 빈 alt는 장식 이미지면 정상이나, **상품 썸네일에 빈 alt는 기회 손실**.\n\n**조치:** 상품 등록/상세 템플릿에서 대표·썸네일 이미지에 `alt=\"브랜드 상품명 컬러\"` 형태로 채우기. 본문 상세컷은 자동 alt(상품명) 규칙 적용.\n\n---\n\n### 🟡 P2. 위생 수준 정리\n\n- **중복 인증 메타**: `naver-site-verification` 4개, `google-site-verification` 3개 — 동작엔 무해하나 head가 지저분. 실제 사용 1~2개만 남기기.\n- **`meta keywords`**: `content=\"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\"` — 구글 무시. 제거하거나 카테고리 키워드로 활용(네이버 일부 참고).\n- **파라미터 URL 중복**: 내비게이션은 `/product/list.html?cate_no=` 사용, 사이트맵은 `/category/슬러그/번호/` 사용. 파라미터 URL이 색인되지 않도록 canonical이 pretty URL을 가리키는지 전수 확인 필요(다음 회차).\n- **사이트맵 저가치 페이지**: `member/agreement.html`, `member/privacy.html` 등 약관 페이지는 사이트맵에서 제외 고려(색인 가치 낮음).\n\n---\n\n## 4. 바로 적용 코드 스니펫\n\n> Cafe24 **쇼핑몰 관리 → 디자인(에디봇/스마트 디자인) → HTML 편집 → `<head>` 공통 영역**에 적용. 적용 전 백업 필수.\n\n### (A) 정리된 Open Graph 블록 — 기존 깨진 커스텀 블록 삭제 후 이것만 유지\n```html\n<meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"website\" />\n<meta property=\"og:site_name\" content=\"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\" />\n<meta property=\"og:title\" content=\"{페이지 타이틀}\" />\n<meta property=\"og:description\" content=\"{페이지 설명}\" />\n<meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"{현재 페이지의 canonical URL}\" />\n<meta property=\"og:image\" content=\"https://(대표도메인)/web/upload/share-image-1-...jpg\" />\n<meta name=\"twitter:card\" content=\"summary_large_image\" />\n```\n※ `blue3200.cafe24.com` 가 들어간 줄, `og:url`에 이미지가 들어간 줄은 **반드시 제거**.\n\n### (B) 통합 Organization + WebSite 구조화 데이터 (Person 블록 대체, 홈에 1회)\n```html\n<script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"Organization\",\n  \"name\": \"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\",\n  \"alternateName\": \"프리미엄멀티샵\",\n  \"url\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/\",\n  \"logo\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/web/upload/(로고이미지).png\",\n  \"sameAs\": [\n    \"https://www.instagram.com/premiummultishop_official/\",\n    \"https://www.facebook.com/premiummultishop/\",\n    \"https://www.youtube.com/channel/UCm0hQKrnpModKlyLaVz4MDQ\"\n  ],\n  \"contactPoint\": {\n    \"@type\": \"ContactPoint\",\n    \"telephone\": \"+82-1588-8410\",\n    \"contactType\": \"customer service\",\n    \"email\": \"info@miracleinvest.group\",\n    \"areaServed\": \"KR\",\n    \"availableLanguage\": \"Korean\"\n  },\n  \"address\": {\n    \"@type\": \"PostalAddress\",\n    \"streetAddress\": \"경기 수원시 권선구 권광로 119\",\n    \"postalCode\": \"16571\",\n    \"addressCountry\": \"KR\"\n  }\n}\n</script>\n<script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"WebSite\",\n  \"name\": \"프리미엄멀티샵\",\n  \"url\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/\",\n  \"potentialAction\": {\n    \"@type\": \"SearchAction\",\n    \"target\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/product/search.html?keyword={search_term_string}\",\n    \"query-input\": \"required name=search_term_string\"\n  }\n}\n</script>\n```\n\n### (C) Product 스키마 보강 (제품 상세 템플릿) — 별점·sku·브레드크럼 추가\n```html\n<script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"Product\",\n  \"name\": \"데우스엑스마키나 쉘 남성 자켓 DMF246595 BLK\",\n  \"sku\": \"DMF246595\",\n  \"image\": [\"https://premiummultishop.com/web/product/big/.../...jpg\"],\n  \"description\": \"...\",\n  \"brand\": { \"@type\": \"Brand\", \"name\": \"Deus Ex Machina\" },\n  \"offers\": {\n    \"@type\": \"AggregateOffer\",\n    \"priceCurrency\": \"KRW\",\n    \"lowPrice\": 299000,\n    \"highPrice\": 299000,\n    \"availability\": \"https://schema.org/InStock\",\n    \"itemCondition\": \"https://schema.org/NewCondition\",\n    \"url\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/product/.../88336/\",\n    \"seller\": { \"@type\": \"Organization\", \"name\": \"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\" }\n  },\n  \"aggregateRating\": {            // ⚠️ 실제 리뷰 수/평점이 있을 때만 출력 (없으면 생략)\n    \"@type\": \"AggregateRating\",\n    \"ratingValue\": \"4.8\",\n    \"reviewCount\": \"37\"\n  }\n}\n</script>\n<script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"BreadcrumbList\",\n  \"itemListElement\": [\n    { \"@type\": \"ListItem\", \"position\": 1, \"name\": \"남성\", \"item\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/category/남성/64/\" },\n    { \"@type\": \"ListItem\", \"position\": 2, \"name\": \"아우터\", \"item\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/category/아우터/.../\" },\n    { \"@type\": \"ListItem\", \"position\": 3, \"name\": \"데우스엑스마키나 쉘 남성 자켓 DMF246595 BLK\" }\n  ]\n}\n</script>\n```\n> ⚠️ `aggregateRating`은 **실제 리뷰 데이터가 있을 때만** 넣어야 합니다. 가짜/빈 값은 구글 수동 조치(penalty) 대상입니다. Cafe24 리뷰 앱의 평점 변수를 연결하세요.\n\n---\n\n## 5. 검증 체크리스트 (적용 후)\n\n- [ ] **GA4**: 실시간 보고서에서 측정ID 1개만 집계되는지 확인\n- [ ] **리치 결과 테스트**: https://search.google.com/test/rich-results 에 제품 URL 입력 → Product/Breadcrumb 통과 확인\n- [ ] **스키마 검증**: https://validator.schema.org/ 로 Organization/WebSite 오류 0 확인\n- [ ] **OG 디버거**: 페이스북 공유 디버거·카카오 공유 미리보기로 이미지/제목 정상 표시\n- [ ] **Search Console**: 대표 도메인 등록 + 사이트맵 제출 + 색인 커버리지 확인\n- [ ] **네이버 서치어드바이저**: 사이트 등록 + 사이트맵 제출 + 웹마스터 진단\n- [ ] **301 확인**: `www` ↔ non-www, http→https 리다이렉트 정상 동작\n\n---\n\n## 6. 다음 회차(루프) 예정 작업\n\n1. **제품/카테고리 대량 크롤** (사이트맵 표본 30~50개) → 중복 title 패턴·thin content·canonical 일관성 전수 점검\n2. **파라미터 URL(`?cate_no=`, 정렬/페이지) canonical 검증** → 중복 색인 위험 정량화\n3. **코어 웹 바이탈/페이지 속도** 신호 점검 (이미지 용량·WebP·lazyload, 리소스 수)\n4. **www/non-www·http 301 리다이렉트** 실제 동작 확인\n5. **내부링크·브레드크럼 구조** 및 카테고리 깊이 분석\n\n---\n\n*본 리포트는 외부에서 접근 가능한 HTML/robots/sitemap을 기준으로 작성됨. 일부 항목(리다이렉트·서버 헤더·실제 리뷰수)은 Cafe24 관리자/서버 확인이 필요하며 ⚠️로 표시함. 리포트 URL이 한글 슬러그 기반으로 SEO 친화적이고, 사이트맵에 **2,710개 URL**(카테고리 1,508 + 제품 1,202)이 등록돼 있으며, 제품 상세에 **Product 구조화 데이터가 이미 존재**하고, 메타 디스크립션이 상품별로 자동 생성되어 품질이 좋습니다. `lang=\"ko\"`, canonical, GA·네이버·구글 인증도 설치돼 있습니다.\n\n그러나 **head 영역에 사이트 전역으로 깔리는 치명적 결함 3가지**가 발견됐고, 이것이 \"유입 정체\"의 숨은 원인일 가능성이 큽니다. 특히 **GA4가 2개 동시 설치**돼 있어 트래픽 측정 자체가 왜곡됐을 수 있습니다 — 즉 \"정체\"라는 진단의 근거 데이터부터 신뢰도 점검이 필요합니다.\n\n### 한눈에 보는 우선순위\n\n| 순위 | 이슈 | 영향 | 난이도 |\n|------|------|------|--------|\n| 🔴 P0 | 중복·깨진 Open Graph 태그 (전역) | SNS 공유·소셜 미리보기 전부 깨짐, 검색엔진에 모순 신호 | 낮음 |\n| 🔴 P0 | GA4 이중 측정 (측정ID 2개) | 트래픽·전환 데이터 신뢰도 붕괴 | 낮음 |\n| 🔴 P0 | JSON-LD `@type:Person` 오류 (전역) | 브랜드를 사람으로 오인식, Organization 신호 분산 | 낮음 |\n| 🟠 P1 | www / non-www 혼재 | 링크 가치 분산, 색인 혼선 | 중간 |\n| 🟠 P1 | Product 스키마 보강 (별점/리뷰·sku·브레드크럼) | 검색결과 별점·리치결과 미노출 = CTR 손실 | 중간 |\n| 🟠 P1 | 카테고리 title 중복(\"신상 - 신상\"), 홈 title 빈약 | 키워드 기회 손실 | 낮음 |\n| 🟠 P1 | 이미지 alt 누락 (상품상세 22/44개) | 이미지 검색(특히 네이버) 유입 손실 | 중간 |\n| 🟡 P2 | 중복 인증태그·meta keywords·파라미터 URL·사이트맵 저가치 페이지 | 위생 수준 | 낮음 |\n\n---\n\n## 2. 잘 되어 있는 점 (유지)\n\n- ✅ **SEO 친화 URL**: `/product/데우스엑스마키나-쉘-남성-자켓-dmf246595-blk/88336/` — 한글 키워드 + 브랜드 + 모델코드 포함. 매우 좋음.\n- ✅ **사이트맵 인덱스 구성**: `sitemap.xml` → `sitemap0/1.xml.gz`, 총 2,710 URL, 최근(2026-06-06) 갱신.\n- ✅ **제품별 메타 디스크립션 자동 생성**: 키워드·정품·배송 메시지 포함, 품질 우수.\n  - 예: *\"PREMIUMMULTISHOP | 데우스엑스마키나 쉘 남성 자켓으로 스타일과 품격을 더하세요. 검증된 정품, 빠른 배송…\"*\n- ✅ **Product 구조화 데이터 존재**: `@type:Product` + `offers`(가격/통화/재고/URL) + `brand`.\n- ✅ **canonical 태그 존재** (홈·제품·카테고리 모두), 제품/카테고리는 pretty URL로 정확히 지정.\n- ✅ **단일 H1** (제품 상세): `<h1>데우스엑스마키나 쉘 남성 자켓 DMF246595 BLK</h1>` — 구조 적절.\n- ✅ `lang=\"ko\"`, viewport, GA4·네이버·구글·페이스북 인증 설치.\n- ✅ robots.txt에 관리/주문/결제/로그인 경로 차단, 네이버(Yeti)·Bing crawl-delay 설정, 사이트맵 명시.\n\n---\n\n## 3. 상세 진단 및 조치\n\n### 🔴 P0-1. 중복·깨진 Open Graph 태그 (전역, 모든 페이지)\n\n모든 페이지 head에 **OG 태그가 두 벌** 들어 있고, 첫 번째(커스텀 삽입) 블록이 깨져 있습니다.\n\n증거 (홈 `home.html` 1336–1342행, 제품 1394–1400행에서 동일 반복):\n```html\n<meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"website\">\n<meta property=\"og:title\" content=\"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\">\n<meta property=\"og:description\" content=\"프리미엄멀티샵은 상품만 판매하는 곳이 아닌, 가치와 신뢰를 판매합니다\">\n<meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"https://blue3200.cafe24.com/web/upload/share-image-1-...png\">  <!-- ❌ og:url에 이미지·스테이징 도메인 -->\n<meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"https://www.premiummultishop.com\"><!-- Cafe24 SEO -->\n```\n문제점:\n1. `og:url`이 **이미지 파일**을 가리킴 (정상값은 페이지 URL).\n2. `og:url`에 **Cafe24 기본 도메인 `blue3200.cafe24.com`**(스테이징/임시 도메인)이 노출 — 외부에 운영 도메인이 아닌 주소가 새어 나감.\n3. 같은 페이지에 `og:title`·`og:url`·`og:type`이 **각각 2~4회 중복**되어 Cafe24 자동 SEO 블록(올바른 값)과 충돌.\n\n**조치:** 디자인 편집(HTML)에서 이 커스텀 OG 블록을 **삭제**하고, Cafe24가 자동 생성하는 `<!-- Cafe24 SEO -->` 블록만 남기세요. (아래 §4 스니펫의 표준 OG 블록 참고)\n\n---\n\n### 🔴 P0-2. GA4 이중 측정 (측정ID 2개 동시 작동)\n\n홈 head에 GA4가 **두 번** 설치돼 서로 다른 측정ID로 동시 전송됩니다.\n```html\n<script async src=\"https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=G-XVP0MPE74Y\"></script>  <!-- 1311행 -->\n<script async src=\"https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=G-CM2XVZ6X45\"></script>  <!-- 1326행 -->\n```\n**영향:** 페이지뷰·세션·전환이 두 속성에 분산/중복 기록됩니다. \"유입 정체\"라는 판단 자체가 잘못된 데이터에서 나왔을 수 있으므로, **SEO 작업 전에 측정부터 정상화**해야 효과를 정확히 검증할 수 있습니다.\n\n**조치:** 실제로 보는 GA4 속성 **하나만 남기고** 나머지 gtag 스니펫 제거. (GTM을 쓰는 경우 GTM 한 곳으로 일원화)\n\n---\n\n### 🔴 P0-3. JSON-LD `@type:Person` 오류 (전역)\n\n전 페이지에 브랜드를 **사람(Person)** 으로 선언한 구조화 데이터가 있습니다.\n```json\n{ \"@context\":\"http://schema.org\", \"@type\":\"Person\", \"name\":\"프리미엄멀티샵\",\n  \"url\":\"https://premiummultishop.com/\",\n  \"sameAs\":[\"instagram…\",\"facebook…\",\"youtube…\"] }\n```\n그리고 **별도로** `@type:Organization` 블록이 또 있습니다(연락처·주소 포함). 즉 회사를 가리키는 신호가 **Person + Organization 두 개로 쪼개져** 있고, 사회관계망(sameAs)은 잘못된 Person 쪽에 붙어 있습니다.\n\n**조치:** Person 블록 삭제 → 하나의 **Organization**(또는 명품 리테일러이므로 `Store`)으로 통합하고 `logo`·`sameAs`를 합치세요. 추가로 **WebSite + SearchAction** 스키마를 넣으면 브랜드 검색 시 사이트링크 검색창이 노출될 수 있습니다. (§4 스니펫)\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 P1-4. www / non-www 도메인 혼재\n\n- 사이트는 **`www.`** 로 접속되는데, canonical·hreflang·og:url은 **non-www**(`https://premiummultishop.com/`)를 가리킴.\n- og:image는 또 `www.` 도메인 사용 → 호스트 표기 불일치.\n\n**영향:** 검색엔진이 정규 호스트를 헷갈리면 링크 평가가 분산될 수 있습니다.\n**조치:**\n1. 대표 도메인 1개 결정(권장: canonical이 가리키는 **non-www** 또는 운영상 쓰는 **www** 중 택1).\n2. 나머지 → 대표 도메인으로 **301 리다이렉트** 강제(Cafe24 멀티도메인/대표도메인 설정).\n3. canonical·og:url·og:image·sitemap 호스트를 **전부 동일하게** 통일.\n4. Search Console·네이버 서치어드바이저에 대표 도메인 등록.\n> ⚠️ 리다이렉트 동작은 서버 설정이라 외부에서 단정 불가 — 실제 301 적용 여부 확인 필요(다음 회차 점검 예정).\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 P1-5. Product 구조화 데이터 보강\n\n현재 Product 스키마(제품 88336)에는 `name·image·description·brand·offers`가 있으나 **리치 결과의 핵심 요소가 빠져** 있습니다.\n\n| 항목 | 현재 | 권장 |\n|------|------|------|\n| `aggregateRating` / `review` | ❌ 없음 | ✅ 추가 (검색결과 **별점** 노출 → CTR↑) |\n| `sku` / `mpn` | ❌ 없음 | ✅ 모델코드(DMF246595) 매핑 |\n| `BreadcrumbList` | ❌ 없음 | ✅ 카테고리 경로 → **브레드크럼 리치결과** |\n| `offers.availability` | `\"InStock\"` | `\"https://schema.org/InStock\"` (정식 표기) |\n| `offers.url` | `?product_no=` 파라미터 | canonical(pretty) URL과 일치 권장 |\n| `itemCondition` | ❌ | `https://schema.org/NewCondition` |\n\n제품 페이지에 **리뷰 섹션(`<h2>리뷰</h2>`)이 실제로 존재**하므로, 집계 평점을 `aggregateRating`으로 연결하면 가장 효과가 큽니다. (§4 스니펫)\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 P1-6. 타이틀 최적화\n\n- **홈 title**: `<title>프리미엄멀티샵</title>` — 브랜드명만. 키워드 0개.\n  - 권장 예: `프리미엄멀티샵 | 명품 편집샵·정품 보장·당일배송`\n- **카테고리 title**: `<title>신상 - 신상 - PREMIUMMULTISHOP</title>` — **\"신상\"이 중복**. 템플릿이 카테고리명을 두 번 출력.\n  - 권장 예: `신상품 | 프리미엄멀티샵` 또는 `신상 명품 신상품 모음 | 프리미엄멀티샵`\n- 제품 title은 양호: `데우스엑스마키나 DMF246595 BLK 남성 자켓 | 프리미엄멀티샵 추천 상품` ✅\n\n**조치:** Cafe24 디자인 편집의 타이틀 템플릿 점검 — 카테고리 중복 변수 제거, 홈 타이틀에 핵심 키워드(명품/편집샵/정품) 반영.\n\n---\n\n### 🟠 P1-7. 이미지 alt 텍스트 누락\n\n| 페이지 | 총 이미지 | alt 속성 자체 없음 | 빈 alt(`alt=\"\"`) |\n|--------|-----------|---------------------|-------------------|\n| 홈 | 209 | 10 | 48 |\n| **제품 상세(88336)** | 44 | **22** | 12 |\n| 카테고리(신상) | 1,018 | 0 | 205 |\n\n- 제품 상세는 **이미지의 절반(22개)에 alt 속성이 아예 없음** — 상세 설명 이미지로 추정. 국내는 **네이버 이미지 검색** 유입 비중이 커서 손실이 큽니다.\n- 빈 alt는 장식 이미지면 정상이나, **상품 썸네일에 빈 alt는 기회 손실**.\n\n**조치:** 상품 등록/상세 템플릿에서 대표·썸네일 이미지에 `alt=\"브랜드 상품명 컬러\"` 형태로 채우기. 본문 상세컷은 자동 alt(상품명) 규칙 적용.\n\n---\n\n### 🟡 P2. 위생 수준 정리\n\n- **중복 인증 메타**: `naver-site-verification` 4개, `google-site-verification` 3개 — 동작엔 무해하나 head가 지저분. 실제 사용 1~2개만 남기기.\n- **`meta keywords`**: `content=\"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\"` — 구글 무시. 제거하거나 카테고리 키워드로 활용(네이버 일부 참고).\n- **파라미터 URL 중복**: 내비게이션은 `/product/list.html?cate_no=` 사용, 사이트맵은 `/category/슬러그/번호/` 사용. 파라미터 URL이 색인되지 않도록 canonical이 pretty URL을 가리키는지 전수 확인 필요(다음 회차).\n- **사이트맵 저가치 페이지**: `member/agreement.html`, `member/privacy.html` 등 약관 페이지는 사이트맵에서 제외 고려(색인 가치 낮음).\n\n---\n\n## 4. 바로 적용 코드 스니펫\n\n> Cafe24 **쇼핑몰 관리 → 디자인(에디봇/스마트 디자인) → HTML 편집 → `<head>` 공통 영역**에 적용. 적용 전 백업 필수.\n\n### (A) 정리된 Open Graph 블록 — 기존 깨진 커스텀 블록 삭제 후 이것만 유지\n```html\n<meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"website\" />\n<meta property=\"og:site_name\" content=\"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\" />\n<meta property=\"og:title\" content=\"{페이지 타이틀}\" />\n<meta property=\"og:description\" content=\"{페이지 설명}\" />\n<meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"{현재 페이지의 canonical URL}\" />\n<meta property=\"og:image\" content=\"https://(대표도메인)/web/upload/share-image-1-...jpg\" />\n<meta name=\"twitter:card\" content=\"summary_large_image\" />\n```\n※ `blue3200.cafe24.com` 가 들어간 줄, `og:url`에 이미지가 들어간 줄은 **반드시 제거**.\n\n### (B) 통합 Organization + WebSite 구조화 데이터 (Person 블록 대체, 홈에 1회)\n```html\n<script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"Organization\",\n  \"name\": \"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\",\n  \"alternateName\": \"프리미엄멀티샵\",\n  \"url\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/\",\n  \"logo\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/web/upload/(로고이미지).png\",\n  \"sameAs\": [\n    \"https://www.instagram.com/premiummultishop_official/\",\n    \"https://www.facebook.com/premiummultishop/\",\n    \"https://www.youtube.com/channel/UCm0hQKrnpModKlyLaVz4MDQ\"\n  ],\n  \"contactPoint\": {\n    \"@type\": \"ContactPoint\",\n    \"telephone\": \"+82-1588-8410\",\n    \"contactType\": \"customer service\",\n    \"email\": \"info@miracleinvest.group\",\n    \"areaServed\": \"KR\",\n    \"availableLanguage\": \"Korean\"\n  },\n  \"address\": {\n    \"@type\": \"PostalAddress\",\n    \"streetAddress\": \"경기 수원시 권선구 권광로 119\",\n    \"postalCode\": \"16571\",\n    \"addressCountry\": \"KR\"\n  }\n}\n</script>\n<script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"WebSite\",\n  \"name\": \"프리미엄멀티샵\",\n  \"url\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/\",\n  \"potentialAction\": {\n    \"@type\": \"SearchAction\",\n    \"target\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/product/search.html?keyword={search_term_string}\",\n    \"query-input\": \"required name=search_term_string\"\n  }\n}\n</script>\n```\n\n### (C) Product 스키마 보강 (제품 상세 템플릿) — 별점·sku·브레드크럼 추가\n```html\n<script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"Product\",\n  \"name\": \"데우스엑스마키나 쉘 남성 자켓 DMF246595 BLK\",\n  \"sku\": \"DMF246595\",\n  \"image\": [\"https://premiummultishop.com/web/product/big/.../...jpg\"],\n  \"description\": \"...\",\n  \"brand\": { \"@type\": \"Brand\", \"name\": \"Deus Ex Machina\" },\n  \"offers\": {\n    \"@type\": \"AggregateOffer\",\n    \"priceCurrency\": \"KRW\",\n    \"lowPrice\": 299000,\n    \"highPrice\": 299000,\n    \"availability\": \"https://schema.org/InStock\",\n    \"itemCondition\": \"https://schema.org/NewCondition\",\n    \"url\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/product/.../88336/\",\n    \"seller\": { \"@type\": \"Organization\", \"name\": \"PREMIUMMULTISHOP\" }\n  },\n  \"aggregateRating\": {            // ⚠️ 실제 리뷰 수/평점이 있을 때만 출력 (없으면 생략)\n    \"@type\": \"AggregateRating\",\n    \"ratingValue\": \"4.8\",\n    \"reviewCount\": \"37\"\n  }\n}\n</script>\n<script type=\"application/ld+json\">\n{\n  \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n  \"@type\": \"BreadcrumbList\",\n  \"itemListElement\": [\n    { \"@type\": \"ListItem\", \"position\": 1, \"name\": \"남성\", \"item\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/category/남성/64/\" },\n    { \"@type\": \"ListItem\", \"position\": 2, \"name\": \"아우터\", \"item\": \"https://premiummultishop.com/category/아우터/.../\" },\n    { \"@type\": \"ListItem\", \"position\": 3, \"name\": \"데우스엑스마키나 쉘 남성 자켓 DMF246595 BLK\" }\n  ]\n}\n</script>\n```\n> ⚠️ `aggregateRating`은 **실제 리뷰 데이터가 있을 때만** 넣어야 합니다. 가짜/빈 값은 구글 수동 조치(penalty) 대상입니다. Cafe24 리뷰 앱의 평점 변수를 연결하세요.\n\n---\n\n## 5. 검증 체크리스트 (적용 후)\n\n- [ ] **GA4**: 실시간 보고서에서 측정ID 1개만 집계되는지 확인\n- [ ] **리치 결과 테스트**: https://search.google.com/test/rich-results 에 제품 URL 입력 → Product/Breadcrumb 통과 확인\n- [ ] **스키마 검증**: https://validator.schema.org/ 로 Organization/WebSite 오류 0 확인\n- [ ] **OG 디버거**: 페이스북 공유 디버거·카카오 공유 미리보기로 이미지/제목 정상 표시\n- [ ] **Search Console**: 대표 도메인 등록 + 사이트맵 제출 + 색인 커버리지 확인\n- [ ] **네이버 서치어드바이저**: 사이트 등록 + 사이트맵 제출 + 웹마스터 진단\n- [ ] **301 확인**: `www` ↔ non-www, http→https 리다이렉트 정상 동작\n\n---\n\n## 6. 다음 회차(루프) 예정 작업\n\n1. **제품/카테고리 대량 크롤** (사이트맵 표본 30~50개) → 중복 title 패턴·thin content·canonical 일관성 전수 점검\n2. **파라미터 URL(`?cate_no=`, 정렬/페이지) canonical 검증** → 중복 색인 위험 정량화\n3. **코어 웹 바이탈/페이지 속도** 신호 점검 (이미지 용량·WebP·lazyload, 리소스 수)\n4. **www/non-www·http 301 리다이렉트** 실제 동작 확인\n5. **내부링크·브레드크럼 구조** 및 카테고리 깊이 분석\n\n---\n\n*본 리포트는 외부에서 접근 가능한 HTML/robots/sitemap을 기준으로 작성됨. 일부 항목(리다이렉트·서버 헤더·실제 리뷰수)은 Cafe24 관리자/서버 확인이 필요하며 ⚠️로 표시함. 리포트 산출물: `premiummultishop-seo/` 폴더.*\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"803e4422-df8f-4123-b626-257413b1ba7a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"C:\\Users\\user\\Desktop\\claude_test\\premiummultishop-seo\\raw","sessionId":"e7d255f2-02c9-446d-84b4-f1caec596bf8","version":"2.1.161","gitBranch":"HEAD"}

{"parentUuid":"a8ca2307-e581-4aef-ad67-eec06fcf1706","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_01DZZu7xUG76C3vVWMZ4pHrz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## 1차 진단 완료 — 요약\n\n`product-page-seo` 스킬 방법론으로 홈·제품상세·카테고리·robots·sitemap을 크롤해 분석했습니다. 전체 리포트: **[SEO-진단리포트-1차.md](premiummultishop-seo/SEO-진단리포트-1차.md)** (수정 코드 스니펫·검증 체크리스트 포함).\n\n**잘 되어 있음:** 한글 슬러그 URL, 사이트맵 2,710개, 제품 Product 스키마 존재, 상품별 메타 디스크립션 품질 우수, canonical·`lang=ko`·인증 설치.\n\n**🔴 즉시 고칠 P0 (모두 사이트 전역 head 결함):**\n1. **중복·깨진 Open Graph** — `og:url`이 이미지+스테이징 도메인(`blue3200.cafe24.com`)을 가리키고, OG 태그가 두 벌 충돌. SNS 공유 미리보기가 깨지고 검색엔진에 모순 신호.\n2. **GA4 이중 설치** (`G-XVP0MPE74Y` + `G-CM2XVZ6X45` 동시 작동) — 트래픽 데이터가 왜곡됨. \"유입 정체\" 판단의 근거 데이터부터 신뢰도 점검 필요.\n3. **JSON-LD `@type:Person` 오류** — 브랜드를 사람으로 선언, Organization 신호 분산.\n\n**🟠 P1:** www/non-www 혼재, Product 스키마에 별점·sku·브레드크럼 추가(검색결과 별점=CTR↑), 카테고리 title 중복(\"신상 - 신상\"), 제품상세 이미지 절반(22/44) alt 누락.\n\n---\n\n자기 페이싱(self-pacing) 모드로 진행합니다. 외부 이벤트 대기는 없으니 Monitor 없이, 약 20분 후 다음 회차를 자동 실행하도록 예약합니다. **다음 회차**에서는 사이트맵 표본 30~50개 제품/카테고리 페이지를 크롤해 중복 title 패턴·canonical 일관성·파라미터 URL 중복·페이지 속도·301 리다이렉트를 점검합니다. {"parentUuid":"56916486-8b4d-4427-a2f0-f2123351a37a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"ac96a533-b469-483c-a37c-f95e9d33bf41","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# /loop — schedule a recurring or self-paced prompt\n\nParse the input below into `[interval] <prompt…>` and schedule it.\n\n## Parsing (in priority order)\n\n1. **Leading token**: if the first whitespace-delimited token matches `^\\d+[smhd]$` (e.g. `5m`, `2h`), that's the interval; the rest is the prompt.\n2. **Trailing \"every\" clause**: otherwise, if the input ends with `every <N><unit>` or `every <N> <unit-word>` (e.g. `every 20m`, `every 5 minutes`, `every 2 hours`), extract that as the interval and strip it from the prompt. Only match when what follows \"every\" is a time expression — `check every PR` has no interval.\n3. **No interval**: otherwise, the entire input is the prompt and you'll self-pace dynamically (see \"Dynamic mode\" below).\n\nIf the resulting prompt is empty, show usage `/loop [interval] <prompt>` and stop.\n\nExamples:\n- `5m /babysit-prs` → interval `5m`, prompt `/babysit-prs` (rule 1)\n- `check the deploy every 20m` → interval `20m`, prompt `check the deploy` (rule 2)\n- `run tests every 5 minutes` → interval `5m`, prompt `run tests` (rule 2)\n- `check the deploy` → no interval → dynamic mode, prompt `check the deploy` (rule 3)\n- `check every PR` → no interval → dynamic mode, prompt `check every PR` (rule 3 — \"every\" not followed by time)\n- `5m` → empty prompt → show usage\n\n## Offer cloud first\n\nBefore any scheduling step, check whether EITHER is true:\n- the parsed interval (rule 1 or 2) is **≥60 minutes**, or\n- regardless of which rule matched, the original input uses daily phrasing (\"every morning\", \"daily\", \"every day\", \"each night\", \"every weekday\")\n\nIf either is true, call AskUserQuestion first:\n- `question`: \"This loop stops when you close this session. Set it up as a cloud schedule instead so it keeps running?\"\n- `header`: \"Schedule\"\n- `options`: `[{label: \"Cloud schedule (recommended)\", description: \"Runs in Anthropic's cloud even after you close this session\"}, {label: \"This session only\", description: \"Runs in this terminal until you exit\"}]`\n\nIf they pick **Cloud schedule**: do NOT call CronCreate. Invoke the `schedule` skill directly via the Skill tool with `args` set to their original input verbatim (e.g. `Skill({skill: \"schedule\", args: \"every morning tell me a joke\"})`), then follow that skill's instructions to completion. Do NOT tell the user to run /schedule themselves. **Then stop — do not continue to any section below** (no CronCreate, no ScheduleWakeup, no \"execute the prompt now\").\nIf they pick **This session only**:\n- If the trigger was a parsed ≥60-minute interval (rule 1 or 2): continue below with that interval.\n- If the trigger was daily phrasing only (rule 3, no parsed interval): do NOT call CronCreate. Explain that a daily-cadence loop won't fire before this session closes, so there's nothing useful to schedule locally — suggest they either pick Cloud schedule, or re-run `/loop` with an explicit shorter interval (e.g. `/loop 1h <prompt>`) if they want a session loop. Then stop.\nIf neither trigger condition was met: continue below.\n\n## Fixed-interval mode (rules 1 and 2)\n\nConvert the interval to a cron expression:\n\n| Interval pattern      | Cron expression     | Notes                                    |\n|-----------------------|---------------------|------------------------------------------|\n| `Nm` where N ≤ 59   | `*/N * * * *`     | every N minutes                          |\n| `Nm` where N ≥ 60   | `0 */H * * *`     | round to hours (H = N/60, must divide 24)|\n| `Nh` where N ≤ 23   | `0 */N * * *`     | every N hours                            |\n| `Nd`                | `0 0 */N * *`     | every N days at midnight local           |\n| `Ns`                | treat as `ceil(N/60)m` | cron minimum granularity is 1 minute  |\n\n**If the interval doesn't cleanly divide its unit** (e.g. `7m` → `*/7 * * * *` gives uneven gaps at :56→:00; `90m` → 1.5h which cron can't express), pick the nearest clean interval and tell the user what you rounded to before scheduling.\n\nThen:\n1. Call CronCreate with: `cron` (the expression above), `prompt` (the parsed prompt verbatim), `recurring: true`.\n2. Briefly confirm: what's scheduled, the cron expression, the human-readable cadence, that recurring tasks auto-expire after 7 days, and that the user can cancel sooner with CronDelete (include the job ID). Only if you did NOT show the cloud-offer AskUserQuestion above (i.e., neither trigger condition applied), end the confirmation with this exact line on its own, italicized: `_Runs until you close this session · For durable cloud-based loops, use /schedule_`. If the user already answered that question, omit this line.\n3. **Then immediately execute the parsed prompt now** — don't wait for the first cron fire. If it's a slash command, invoke it via the Skill tool; otherwise act on it directly.\n\n## Dynamic mode (rule 3 — no interval)\n\nThe user wants you to self-pace. Decide what makes the next iteration worth running — a passage of time, or an observable event.\n\n1. **Run the parsed prompt now.** If it's a slash command, invoke it via the Skill tool; otherwise act on it directly.\n2. **If the next run is gated on an event** (CI finishing, a log line matching, a file changing, a PR comment) and no Monitor is already running for it: arm one now with `persistent: true`. Its events arrive as `<task-notification>` messages and wake this loop immediately — you do not wait for the ScheduleWakeup deadline. Arm once; on later iterations call TaskList first and skip this step if a monitor is already running.\n3. **Briefly confirm**: that you're self-pacing, whether a Monitor is the primary wake signal, that you ran the task now, and what fallback delay you're about to pick. Write this as text *before* calling ScheduleWakeup — the turn ends as soon as that tool returns.\n4. **Then, as the last action of this turn, call ScheduleWakeup** with:\n   - `delaySeconds`: with a Monitor armed this is the **fallback heartbeat** — how long to wait if no event fires (lean 1200–1800s; idle ticks past the 5-minute cache window are pure overhead). Without a Monitor this is the cadence — pick based on what you observed. Read the tool's own description for cache-aware delay guidance.\n   - `reason`: one short sentence on why you picked that delay.\n   - `prompt`: the full original /loop input verbatim, prefixed with `/loop ` so the next firing re-enters this skill and continues the loop. For example, if the user typed `/loop check the deploy`, pass `/loop check the deploy` as the prompt.\n5. **If you were woken by a `<task-notification>`** rather than this prompt: handle the event in the context of the loop task, then call ScheduleWakeup again with the same `prompt` and the same 1200–1800s `delaySeconds` from step 4 — the Monitor remains the wake signal; this only resets the safety net.\n6. **To stop the loop**, omit the ScheduleWakeup call and TaskStop any Monitor you armed (use TaskList to find the task ID if it is no longer in context).\n\n## Input\n\npremiummultishop.com 사이트에 대해 product-page-seo 스킬로 SEO를 수행. 내부 제품 페이지와 사이트 구석구석을 계속 살펴보며 SEO 개선 작업 진행 (다음 회차: 리포트의 \"다음 회차 예정 작업\" 섹션부터 — 제품/카테고리 표본 30~50개 크롤, 중복 title·canonical·파라미터 URL·페이지 속도·301 리다이렉트 점검 후 premiummultishop-seo 폴더에 2차 결과 추가)"}]},"isMeta":true,"uuid":"694e4db4-38bd-4099-83c3-68f0b241c957","timestamp":"2026-06-08T04:44:05.880Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"C:\\Users\\user\\Desktop\\claude_test","sessionId":"e7d255f2-02c9-446d-84b4-f1caec596bf8","version":"2.1.161","gitBranch":"HEAD"}

{"parentUuid":"f052d746-e96c-4bc0-b78f-f19b8e07c4da","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_016Ad5UmgoyN8ywAJgk7YRi6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HHJJzpakxG4DpXKD18qNBH","name":"PowerShell","input":{"command":"[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol=[Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12\n$ua='Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36'\n$base='C:\\Users\\user\\Desktop\\claude_test\\premiummultishop-seo'\n$raw=Join-Path $base 'raw'\nfunction Get-Loc($file){ ([regex]::Matches((Get-Content $file -Raw),'<loc>([^<]+)</loc>') | ForEach-Object { $_.Groups[1].Value }) }\nfunction Sample($arr,$n){ if($arr.Count -le $n){return $arr}; $step=[math]::Floor($arr.Count/$n); 0..($n-1) | ForEach-Object { $arr[$_*$step] } }\n$prod = Sample (Get-Loc (Join-Path $raw 'sitemap0.xml') | Where-Object {$_ -match '/product/'}) 8\n$prod2= Sample (Get-Loc (Join-Path $raw 'sitemap1.xml')) 26\n$cats = Sample (Get-Loc (Join-Path $raw 'sitemap0.xml') | Where-Object {$_ -match '/category/'}) 12\n$params = @('https://www.premiummultishop.com/product/list.html?cate_no=25',\n           'https://www.premiummultishop.com/product/detail.html?product_no=88336',\n           'https://www.premiummultishop.com/product/list.html?cate_no=25&sort_method=5',\n           'https://www.premiummultishop.com/category/신상/25/?page=2')\n$all = @()\n$all += ($prod + $prod2 | Select-Object -Unique) | ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{type='product';url=$_} }\n$all += $cats | ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{type='category';url=$_} }\n$all += $params | ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{type='param';url=$_} }\n$rows=@()\nforeach($it in $all){\n  try{\n    $r=Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $it.url -UserAgent $ua -Headers @{'Accept-Language'='ko-KR,ko'} -TimeoutSec 35 -UseBasicParsing\n    $h=$r.Content\n    $title = if($h -match '(?s)<title>(.*?)</title>'){$matches[1].Trim()}else{''}\n    $can   = if($h -match 'rel=\"canonical\"\\s+href=\"([^\"]+)\"'){$matches[1]}else{''}\n    $brokenOG = [bool]($h -match 'blue3200\\.cafe24\\.com')\n    $dupOG = ([regex]::Matches($h,'property=\"og:title\"')).Count\n    $rows += [pscustomobject]@{type=$it.type; {"parentUuid":"c920a31f-400f-4194-bf1a-468196de0ec7","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_01LLuL7NphWaG4W4eKS7j6rM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"**Pass-2 findings so far:**\n- ✅ **Param URLs canonicalize well**: `?cate_no=`, `?product_no=`, `?sort_method=` all point to pretty canonical URLs.\n- ⚠️ **Pagination problem**: `?page=2` canonicalizes to **page 1** (`/category/신상/25/`), not to itself — a known anti-pattern that can leave deep-catalog products under-indexed.\n- 🔴 **Broken/duplicate OG confirmed on 50/50 pages** — fully sitewide.\n\nNow let me analyze title duplication patterns and page-speed signals."}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2836,"cache_read_input_tokens":92137,"output_tokens":1780,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2836,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1780,"cache_read_input_tokens":92137,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2836,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2836},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CbqBknSmCUHkNkaMvhRvT","attributionSkill":"loop","type":"assistant","uuid":"e3198550-1963-4f87-a278-31327d3a6d8d","timestamp":"2026-06-08T04:47:28.089Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"C:\\Users\\user\\Desktop\\claude_test","sessionId":"e7d255f2-02c9-446d-84b4-f1caec596bf8","version":"2.1.161","gitBranch":"HEAD"}
